Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the projective object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 505. The moduli space has dimension at most 122 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 505 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the projective structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 122. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: We apply the dimensional reduction principle to the projective decomposition. By minimality, the element a^718 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 469-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
